- **debug:kubernetes**: Debug Kubernetes clusters and workloads systematically with this comprehensive troubleshooting skill. Covers CrashLoopBackOff, ImagePullBackOff, OOMKilled, pending pods, service connectivity issues, PVC binding failures, and RBAC permission errors.
